# UNIB script and data catalogue

Generated by `tools/squirrel/catalog.py unib <data dir>`; do not edit by hand. JSON: `unib_files.json`, `unib_names.json`. Translations are machine (`"tr":"machine"`) until reviewed; JP is kept beside them.

## Shared state names

| state | engine consts | def_ consts | MvStatus fields | std moves (first) |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| stand (standing) | BCMDConst._PosState_Stand=1, BMvConst._PosState_Stand=1 | def_PAT_Standby, def_PAT_Standby_1, def_PAT_Standby_2 |  | Mv_Standby, Mv_VGuard_Stand, Mv_VGuard_StandEnd |
| crouch (crouching) | BCMDConst._PosState_Crouch=4, BMvConst._PosState_Crouch=4 | def_FC_WallRecover_Sita, def_PAT_Crouch, def_PAT_Crouch_Wait, def_PAT_Crouch_toN |  | Mv_Crouch, Mv_Crouch_Wait, Mv_Crouch_toN, Mv_VGuard_Crouch, Mv_VGuard_CrouchEnd, Mv_WallRecover_Jump_Sita |
| air (airborne) | BCMDConst._PosState_Air=2, BMvConst._PosState_Air=2 | def_FL_AtkAirA_LandRecovery, def_FL_AtkAirA_LandRecovery_Assaut, def_FL_AtkAirA_LandRecovery_AssautShielded, def_FL_AtkAirA_LandRecovery_Shielded, def_FL_AtkAirB_LandRecovery, def_FL_AtkAirB_LandRecovery_Assaut | isLanding | Mv_AssaultCansel_Air, Mv_Assault_Air, Mv_Atk_Air2A, Mv_Atk_Air2B, Mv_Atk_Air2C, Mv_Atk_Air4A |
